## Config: FabConfig (ModConfig.Type.LOCAL registered in EUVCRAFTMod.java, runtime run/config/lithmod-local.toml)

Parsed across FabConfig.java multiline BUILDER chains; defaults for FabSettings.* references are resolved from the parsed FabSettings.java constants. 15 keys total.

| Category | Key | Field | Default | Range |
|----------|-----|-------|---------|-------|
| general | foupCapacity | FOUP_CAPACITY | FabSettings.DEFAULT_FOUP_CAPACITY (=25) | [1, 64] |
| general | wafersPerBoule | WAFERS_PER_BOULE | FabSettings.DEFAULT_WAFERS_PER_BOULE (=25) | [1, 64] |
| general | chipsPerWafer | CHIPS_PER_WAFER | FabSettings.DEFAULT_CHIPS_PER_WAFER (=4) | [1, 64] |
| general | maskUses | MASK_USES | FabSettings.DEFAULT_MASK_USES (=4) | [1, 1000] |
| general | resistPerLayer | RESIST_PER_LAYER | FabSettings.DEFAULT_RESIST_PER_LAYER (=1) | [0, 64] |
| general | rollsPerLayer | ROLLS_PER_LAYER | FabSettings.DEFAULT_ROLLS_PER_LAYER (=1) | [1, 8] |
| general | yieldMultiplier | YIELD_MULTIPLIER | FabSettings.DEFAULT_YIELD_MULTIPLIER (=1.0) | [0.1, 2.0] |
| general | tierYieldOverrides | TIER_YIELD_OVERRIDES | List.of() | list of strings, empty allowed |
| general | layerTicksOverrides | LAYER_TICKS_OVERRIDES | List.of() | list of strings, empty allowed |
| compute | coreCountOptions | CORE_COUNT_OPTIONS | FabSettings.DEFAULT_CORE_COUNT_OPTIONS (=List.of(1, 2, 4, 8, 12, 24)) | list of integers, each in [1, 256] |
| compute | comfortableCoreOverrides | COMFORTABLE_CORE_OVERRIDES | List.of() | list of strings, empty allowed |
| compute | coreCountPenaltySteepness | CORE_COUNT_PENALTY_STEEPNESS | FabSettings.DEFAULT_CORE_COUNT_PENALTY_STEEPNESS (=2.0) | [0.0, 10.0] |
| features | tier6Enabled | TIER_6_ENABLED | FabSettings.DEFAULT_TIER_6_ENABLED (=true) | boolean (ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue) |
| features | debugCommands | DEBUG_COMMANDS | FabSettings.DEFAULT_DEBUG_COMMANDS (=false) | boolean (ModConfigSpec.BooleanValue) |
| power | fePowerMultiplier | FE_POWER_MULTIPLIER | FabSettings.DEFAULT_FE_POWER_MULTIPLIER (=1.0) | [0.0, 100.0] |

Actual ranges come from the defineInRange min/max literals, the list validators in FabConfig, and the FabSettings clamp constants; list overrides use the Tn=value format parsed by FabSettings.parseTierOverrideList.

### Machine recipes (data/lithmod/machine_recipes/*.json)

Loaded by MachineRecipeLoader (directory string "machine_recipes") on every server resource reload; priority is file-id order. energy_per_tick is optional and falls back to the machine definition.

| Path | machine | result | count | ticks | energy_per_tick | Gate |
|------|---------|--------|-------|-------|-----------------|------|

## Data Files and Formats (actual directories and loader declarations)

| Loader | data/lithmod directory (parsed from loader source) | Tracked source path |
|--------|------------------------------------------------------|---------------------|
| DieRulesDataLoader | data/lithmod/die_rules | src/main/java/com/euvcraft/lithmod/mc/data/DieRulesDataLoader.java |
| MachineDefinitionLoader | data/lithmod/machines | src/main/java/com/euvcraft/lithmod/mc/data/MachineDefinitionLoader.java |
| MachineRecipeLoader | data/lithmod/machine_recipes | src/main/java/com/euvcraft/lithmod/mc/data/MachineRecipeLoader.java |
| SupportLineDataLoader | data/lithmod/support_lines | src/main/java/com/euvcraft/lithmod/mc/data/SupportLineDataLoader.java |
| TierDataLoader | data/lithmod/tiers | src/main/java/com/euvcraft/lithmod/mc/data/TierDataLoader.java |

Tier JSON fields per file (raw literals read from src/main/resources/data/lithmod/tiers):

| File | id | index | layers | layer_ticks | default_yield_percent | optional |
|------|----|-------|--------|-------------|----------------------|----------|
| data/lithmod/tiers/t1.json | T1 | 1 | 1 | 600 | 60.0 | False |
| data/lithmod/tiers/t2.json | T2 | 2 | 12 | 100 | 80.0 | False |
| data/lithmod/tiers/t3.json | T3 | 3 | 24 | 100 | 90.0 | False |
| data/lithmod/tiers/t4.json | T4 | 4 | 32 | 100 | 92.0 | False |
| data/lithmod/tiers/t5.json | T5 | 5 | 42 | 100 | 93.0 | False |
| data/lithmod/tiers/t6.json | T6 | 6 | 70 | 100 | 85 | True |
| data/lithmod/tiers/t7.json | T7 | 7 | 80 | 100 | 85.0 | False |
| data/lithmod/tiers/t8.json | T8 | 8 | 90 | 100 | 80.0 | False |


M0-owned data files stay as tracked: data/lithmod/die_rules/rules.json, data/lithmod/support_lines/matrix.json, and the 8 data/lithmod/tiers/t<index>.json files. Block loot tables: 112 files under data/lithmod/loot_table/blocks; block tag files: 2. Counts are read at generation time.

### Machine data codecs (actual codec sources; core records stay game-independent)

#### src/main/java/com/euvcraft/lithmod/mc/data/MachineDefinitionCodec.java

```java
package com.euvcraft.lithmod.mc.data;

import com.euvcraft.lithmod.core.*;
import com.mojang.serialization.Codec;
import com.mojang.serialization.codecs.RecordCodecBuilder;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;

/** Minecraft serialization adapter; the core record stays independent of game libraries. */
public final class MachineDefinitionCodec {
    private MachineDefinitionCodec() { }

    public static final Codec<MachineDefinition> CODEC = RecordCodecBuilder.create(instance -> instance.group(
        Codec.STRING.fieldOf("id").forGetter(MachineDefinition::id),
        Codec.INT.fieldOf("energy_capacity").forGetter(MachineDefinition::energyCapacity),
        Codec.INT.fieldOf("max_receive").forGetter(MachineDefinition::maxReceive),
        Codec.INT.fieldOf("energy_per_tick").forGetter(MachineDefinition::energyPerTick),
        Codec.unboundedMap(Codec.STRING, Codec.INT)
            .optionalFieldOf("per_tier_energy_per_tick", Map.of()).forGetter(MachineDefinition::perTierEnergyPerTick),
        Codec.unboundedMap(Codec.STRING, Codec.INT)
            .optionalFieldOf("params", Map.of()).forGetter(MachineDefinition::params)
    ).apply(instance, MachineDefinition::new));

    public static final Codec<java.util.List<MachineDefinition>> LIST_CODEC = CODEC.listOf();
}
```

#### src/main/java/com/euvcraft/lithmod/mc/data/MachineRecipeSpecCodec.java

```java
package com.euvcraft.lithmod.mc.data;

import com.euvcraft.lithmod.core.*;
import com.mojang.serialization.Codec;
import com.mojang.serialization.codecs.RecordCodecBuilder;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;

/** Minecraft serialization adapter; the core record stays independent of game libraries. */
public final class MachineRecipeSpecCodec {
    private MachineRecipeSpecCodec() { }

    public static final Codec<MachineRecipeSpec> CODEC = RecordCodecBuilder.create(instance -> instance.group(
        Codec.STRING.fieldOf("machine").forGetter(MachineRecipeSpec::machineId),
        MachineIngredientCodec.FLEXIBLE_CODEC.listOf().fieldOf("ingredients").forGetter(MachineRecipeSpec::ingredients),
        MachineResultCodec.CODEC.fieldOf("result").forGetter(MachineRecipeSpec::result),
        Codec.INT.fieldOf("ticks").forGetter(MachineRecipeSpec::ticks),
        Codec.INT.optionalFieldOf("energy_per_tick")
            .forGetter(spec -> Optional.ofNullable(spec.energyPerTickOverride()))
    ).apply(instance, (machine, ingredients, result, ticks, energyPerTick) ->
        new MachineRecipeSpec(machine, ingredients, result, ticks, energyPerTick.orElse(null))));

    public static final Codec<List<MachineRecipeSpec>> LIST_CODEC = CODEC.listOf();
}
```

#### src/main/java/com/euvcraft/lithmod/mc/data/MachineIngredientCodec.java

```java
package com.euvcraft.lithmod.mc.data;

import com.euvcraft.lithmod.core.*;
import com.mojang.serialization.Codec;
import com.mojang.serialization.codecs.RecordCodecBuilder;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;

/** Minecraft serialization adapter; the core record stays independent of game libraries. */
public final class MachineIngredientCodec {
    private MachineIngredientCodec() { }

    public static final Codec<MachineIngredient> CODEC = RecordCodecBuilder.create(instance -> instance.group(
        Codec.STRING.fieldOf("item").forGetter(ingredient -> ingredient.id()),
        Codec.INT.optionalFieldOf("count", 1).forGetter(MachineIngredient::count)
    ).apply(instance, MachineIngredient::item));

    public static final Codec<MachineIngredient> TAG_CODEC = RecordCodecBuilder.create(instance -> instance.group(
        Codec.STRING.fieldOf("tag").forGetter(ingredient -> ingredient.id()),
        Codec.INT.optionalFieldOf("count", 1).forGetter(MachineIngredient::count)
    ).apply(instance, MachineIngredient::tag));

    /**
     * Accepts either {@code {"item": ..., "count": ...}} or {@code {"tag": ..., "count": ...}},
     * so a datapack author picks the key that matches the shape of the ingredient. The item
     * form is tried first and falls through to the tag form when it does not apply.
     */
    public static final Codec<MachineIngredient> FLEXIBLE_CODEC =
        Codec.withAlternative(CODEC, TAG_CODEC);

}
```

#### src/main/java/com/euvcraft/lithmod/mc/data/MachineResultCodec.java

```java
package com.euvcraft.lithmod.mc.data;

import com.euvcraft.lithmod.core.*;
import com.mojang.serialization.Codec;
import com.mojang.serialization.codecs.RecordCodecBuilder;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import com.google.gson.JsonElement;
import com.google.gson.JsonParser;
import com.mojang.serialization.DataResult;
import com.mojang.serialization.Dynamic;
import com.mojang.serialization.JsonOps;

/** Minecraft serialization adapter; the core record stays independent of game libraries. */
public final class MachineResultCodec {
    private MachineResultCodec() { }

    /**
     * Reads one arbitrary JSON value and keeps it as compact text.
     *
     * <p>Written with explicit types because the inference through
     * {@code Codec.PASSTHROUGH.comapFlatMap} does not resolve on its own.</p>
     */
    private static final Codec<String> JSON_TEXT_CODEC =
        Codec.PASSTHROUGH.flatComapMap(
            (Dynamic<?> dynamic) -> dynamic.convert(JsonOps.INSTANCE).getValue().toString(),
            (String text) -> DataResult.success(
                new Dynamic<JsonElement>(JsonOps.INSTANCE, JsonParser.parseString(text))));

    public static final Codec<MachineResult> CODEC = RecordCodecBuilder.create(instance -> instance.group(
        Codec.STRING.fieldOf("item").forGetter(MachineResult::itemId),
        Codec.INT.optionalFieldOf("count", 1).forGetter(MachineResult::count),
        Codec.unboundedMap(Codec.STRING, JSON_TEXT_CODEC)
            .optionalFieldOf("components", Map.of()).forGetter(MachineResult::components)
    ).apply(instance, MachineResult::new));


}
```

MachineIngredient FLEXIBLE_CODEC accepts ("item", count) or ("tag", count); MachineResultCodec keeps result components as arbitrary JSON via a PASSTHROUGH codec (protocol object stays unquoted on disk).

## Valid Local Resource Paths (verified directories)

| Kind | Root-relative pattern |
|------|-----------------------|
| Machine Definition | data/lithmod/machines/<id>.json |
| Machine Recipe | data/lithmod/machine_recipes/<name>.json |
| Crafting Recipe | data/lithmod/recipe/crafting/<name>.json |
| Cooking Recipe (smelting/blasting) | data/lithmod/recipe/<name>.json |
| Tier Data | data/lithmod/tiers/t<index>.json |
| Die Rules | data/lithmod/die_rules/<name>.json |
| Support Lines | data/lithmod/support_lines/<name>.json |
| Block Loot Tables | data/lithmod/loot_table/blocks/<block>.json |
| Block Tags | data/lithmod/tags/block/machine.json + data/minecraft/tags/block/mineable/pickaxe.json, needs_stone_tool.json |
| Runtime Config | run/config/lithmod-local.toml (ModConfig.Type.LOCAL, registered in EUVCRAFTMod.java) |
| Fixture gate | JVM system property lithmodFixtures AND non-production (GameTestFixtures.java); -PlithmodFixtures=false with -PlithmodShippingProbe=true registers one off-mode case |

## Open/Decided Design Decisions (owner choices with tracked report references)

No resolved questions are left open and no decisions are guessed. Every referenced file is a tracked path in this repository and its existence is checked at generation time.

| Decision | Status | Source Reference |
|----------|--------|------------------|
| Silicon cooking ingredient is minecraft:quartz (owner QUARTZ decision; sand smelts to glass is vanilla-only) | Decided | docs/reports/M1-04_silicon-chain.md |
| Pattern Generator machine recipes consume blank_mask plus copper/iron/redstone common metals | Decided | docs/reports/M1-11_review.md |
| Resist top-ups while running/paused: same item + same components only; extraction/swap/creative clone locked | Decided | docs/reports/M1-09_process-cell-controller.md; docs/reports/M1-09_acceptance.md |
| Abort discards unfinished progress without refund, retains completed layers, and latches until the FOUP is removed and reinserted | Decided | docs/reports/M1-09_process-cell-controller.md; docs/reports/M1-12_baseline-audit.md |
| Process Cell geometry: StructureShape(2,2,3) = two blocks wide, two tall, three long (12 members; 1 controller, 1 or 2 power ports) | Decided | docs/reports/M1-11_acceptance.md; docs/reports/M1-12_baseline-audit.md |
| M1 source takes precedence over older references (owner-confirmed); concrete M1-12 constraints tabled in the baseline audit | Decided | docs/reports/M1-12_baseline-audit.md |
| T6 core choices: later owner decision, out of M1-12 scope; features.tier6Enabled=false skips T6 (tiers go T5 -> T7) | Decided | docs/reports/M1-12_baseline-audit.md; docs/reports/M0-02_config-system.md |

## 26.3 API Notes from M1 Reports

Registration, energy, inventory persistence and menu APIs established by [M1-02s energy spike](reports/M1-02s_energy-spike.md):

| Topic | Verified API | Original source evidence |
|-------|--------------|--------------------------|
| Block registration | `DeferredRegister.createBlocks(String)`, `DeferredRegister.Blocks.registerBlock(String, Function<Properties,B>, UnaryOperator<Properties>)` returning `DeferredBlock<B>` | `net/zagdrath/arcforge/registry/ModBlocks.java` line 153 and line 166: `BLOCKS.registerBlock("firebox", FireboxBlock::new, p -> machineProperties(p, 13))` |
| Inventory persistence | `net.minecraft.world.ContainerHelper.loadAllItems(ValueInput, NonNullList<ItemStack>)` / `saveAllItems(ValueOutput, NonNullList<ItemStack>)` | `net/zagdrath/encodedlogistics/blockentity/LithographyPressBlockEntity.java` lines 205 and 212 |
| Menu type registration | `DeferredRegister.create(Registries.MENU, String)`; `IMenuTypeExtension.create(IContainerFactory<T>)` returning `new MenuType<>(factory, FeatureFlags.DEFAULT_FLAGS)` | `net/zagdrath/arcforge/registry/ModMenuTypes.java` line 75 and line 106; `net/neoforged/neoforge/common/extensions/IMenuTypeExtension.java` |
| Menu abstract methods | `stillValid(Player)` and `quickMoveStack(Player, int)` are both abstract | found by compiling; `javap` on `AbstractContainerMenu` in the patched 26.3 jar |
